(herald "M8 investigation: lemma_session_m6" (bound 30))

(comment "CPSA 4.4.9")
(comment "All input read from lemma_session_m6.scm")
(comment "Strand count bounded at 30")

(defprotocol tls13mtlspfs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (cpriv-stor locn))
    (trace (load cpriv-stor (pv c cpk (invk cpk)))
      (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x)
    (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-client-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "client" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-client-0
    (forall ((z strd) (c name) (cpk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c)) (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))

(defskeleton tls13mtlspfs
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (cpk spk akey)
    (self s ca c name) (pt pt-0 pt-1 pval) (priv-stor priv-stor-0 locn)
    (y rndx) (x expt))
  (defstrand server 6 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (ca ca) (c c)
    (priv-stor priv-stor-0) (y y) (x x))
  (deflistener (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
  (defstrand privkey-disclose 3 (pk cpk) (self self)
    (priv-stor priv-stor))
  (precedes ((0 5) (2 0)))
  (uniq-orig sr)
  (uniq-gen y)
  (absent (y x))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (cpk akey) (self name) (priv-stor locn) (y rndx)
        (x expt) (z z-0 z-1 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z 6) (p "" z-0 2) (p "privkey-disclose" z-1 3)
          (p "server" "cr" z cr) (p "server" "sr" z sr)
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "y" z y)
          (p "server" "x" z x) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z-1 cpk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z-1 self)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "priv-stor" z-1 priv-stor)
          (prec z 5 z-1 0) (ugen y) (uniq-at sr z 2)) (false))))
  (traces
    ((load priv-stor-0 (cat pt (pv s spk (invk spk))))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    ((recv (exp (gen) (mul y x))) (send (exp (gen) (mul y x))))
    ((load priv-stor (cat pt-0 (pv self cpk (invk cpk))))
      (stor priv-stor (cat pt-1 "nil")) (send cpk)))
  (label 0)
  (unrealized (1 0))
  (preskeleton)
  (maps)
  (origs (pt-1 (2 1)) (sr (0 2)))
  (ugens (y (0 2)))
  (comment "Not a skeleton"))

          (cat c (invk cpk)

            (hash "finished"

(defskeleton tls13mtlspfs
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (cpk spk akey)
    (self s ca c name) (pt pt-0 pt-1 pval) (priv-stor priv-stor-0 locn)
    (y rndx) (x expt))
  (defstrand server 6 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (ca ca) (c c)
    (priv-stor priv-stor-0) (y y) (x x))
  (deflistener (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
  (defstrand privkey-disclose 3 (pk cpk) (self self)
    (priv-stor priv-stor))
  (precedes ((0 2) (1 0)) ((0 5) (2 0)))
  (uniq-orig sr)
  (uniq-gen y)
  (absent (y x))
  (gen-st (pv self cpk (invk cpk)) (pv s spk (invk spk)))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (cpk akey) (self name) (priv-stor locn) (y rndx)
        (x expt) (z z-0 z-1 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z 6) (p "" z-0 2) (p "privkey-disclose" z-1 3)
          (p "server" "cr" z cr) (p "server" "sr" z sr)
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "y" z y)
          (p "server" "x" z x) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z-1 cpk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z-1 self)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "priv-stor" z-1 priv-stor)
          (prec z 5 z-1 0) (ugen y) (uniq-at sr z 2)) (false))))
  (rule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0 trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1)
  (traces
    ((load priv-stor-0 (cat pt (pv s spk (invk spk))))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))

              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s

                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s

            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s

(defprotocol tls13mtlspfs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (cpriv-stor locn))
    (trace (load cpriv-stor (pv c cpk (invk cpk)))
      (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x)
    (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-client-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "client" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-client-0
    (forall ((z strd) (c name) (cpk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c)) (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))

(defskeleton tls13mtlspfs
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (cpk spk akey)
    (self s ca c name) (pt pt-0 pt-1 pval) (priv-stor priv-stor-0 locn)
    (y rndx) (x expt))
  (defstrand server 6 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (ca ca) (c c)

  (defstrand privkey-disclose 3 (pk cpk) (self self)
    (priv-stor priv-stor))
  (precedes ((0 5) (2 0)))
  (uniq-orig sr)
  (uniq-gen y)
  (absent (y x))
  (goals

        (and (p "server" z 6) (p "" z-0 2) (p "privkey-disclose" z-1 3)
          (p "server" "cr" z cr) (p "server" "sr" z sr)
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "y" z y)
          (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "" "x" z-0
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z-1 cpk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z-1 self)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "priv-stor" z-1 priv-stor)
          (prec z 5 z-1 0) (ugen y) (uniq-at sr z 2)) (false))))
  (traces
    ((load priv-stor-0 (cat pt (pv s spk (invk spk))))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))

              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    ((recv (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
      (send (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
    ((load priv-stor (cat pt-0 (pv self cpk (invk cpk))))
      (stor priv-stor (cat pt-1 "nil")) (send cpk)))

(defprotocol tls13mtlspfs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (cpriv-stor locn))
    (trace (load cpriv-stor (pv c cpk (invk cpk)))
      (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x)
    (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-client-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "client" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-client-0
    (forall ((z strd) (c name) (cpk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c)) (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))

(defskeleton tls13mtlspfs
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (cpk spk akey)
    (self s ca c name) (pt pt-0 pt-1 pval) (priv-stor priv-stor-0 locn)
    (y rndx) (x expt))

          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    ((recv (cat (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
      (send (cat (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
    ((load priv-stor (cat pt (pv self cpk (invk cpk))))
      (stor priv-stor (cat pt-0 "nil")) (send cpk))
    ((load priv-stor (cat pt-1 ignore))
      (stor priv-stor (cat pt (pv self cpk (invk cpk))))))
  (label 16)
  (parent 15)
  (realized)
  (shape)
  (satisfies
    (no (fact false) (cr cr) (sr sr) (cpk cpk) (self self)
      (priv-stor priv-stor) (y y) (x x) (z 0) (z-0 1) (z-1 2)))
  (maps
    ((0 1 2)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (cpk cpk) (self self) (priv-stor priv-stor) (y y)
        (x x) (s self) (ca ca) (c c) (spk cpk) (request request)
        (response response) (priv-stor-0 priv-stor))))
  (origs (pt (3 1)) (pt-0 (2 1)) (sr (0 2)))
  (ugens (cpk (3 1)) (y (0 2))))

(defskeleton tls13mtlspfs
  (vars (ignore ignore-0 mesg) (cr sr random32) (request response text)
    (cpk spk akey) (self s ca c name) (pt pt-0 pt-1 pt-2 pt-3 pval)
    (priv-stor priv-stor-0 locn) (y rndx) (x expt))
  (defstrand server 6 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (ca ca) (c c)
    (priv-stor priv-stor-0) (y y) (x x))
  (deflistener (cat (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
  (defstrand privkey-disclose 3 (pk cpk) (self self)
    (priv-stor priv-stor))
  (defstrand cert-gen 2 (ignore ignore) (pk cpk) (self self)
    (priv-stor priv-stor))
  (defstrand cert-gen 2 (ignore ignore-0) (pk spk) (self s)
    (priv-stor priv-stor-0))
  (precedes ((0 2) (1 0)) ((0 5) (2 0)) ((3 1) (0 3)) ((4 1) (0 0)))
  (uniq-orig sr)
  (uniq-gen cpk spk y)
  (absent (y x))
  (gen-st (pv self cpk (invk cpk)) (pv s spk (invk spk)))
  (leads-to ((3 1) (2 0)) ((4 1) (0 0)))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (cpk akey) (self name) (priv-stor locn) (y rndx)
        (x expt) (z z-0 z-1 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z 6) (p "" z-0 2) (p "privkey-disclose" z-1 3)
          (p "server" "cr" z cr) (p "server" "sr" z sr)
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "y" z y)
          (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "" "x" z-0
            (cat (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z-1 cpk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z-1 self)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "priv-stor" z-1 priv-stor)
          (prec z 5 z-1 0) (ugen y) (uniq-at sr z 2)) (false))))
  (rule trRl_cert-gen-at-0 trRl_cert-gen-at-1)
  (operation channel-test (added-strand cert-gen 2)
    (ch-msg priv-stor-0 (cat pt (pv s spk (invk spk)))) (0 0))
  (strand-map 0 1 2 3)
  (traces
    ((load priv-stor-0 (cat pt (pv s spk (invk spk))))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))

              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)

              (hash "finished"

                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s

(defprotocol tls13mtlspfs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (cpriv-stor locn))
    (trace (load cpriv-stor (pv c cpk (invk cpk)))
      (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x)
    (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-client-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "client" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-client-0
    (forall ((z strd) (c name) (cpk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c)) (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))

              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    ((recv (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
      (send (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr)))
    ((load priv-stor (cat pt-0 (pv self cpk (invk cpk))))
      (stor priv-stor (cat pt-1 "nil")) (send cpk))
    ((load priv-stor (cat pt-2 ignore))
      (stor priv-stor (cat pt-0 (pv self cpk (invk cpk)))))
    ((load priv-stor-0 (cat pt-3 ignore-0))
      (stor priv-stor-0 (cat pt (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (label 23)
  (parent 21)
  (realized)
  (shape)
  (satisfies
    (no (fact false) (cr cr) (sr sr) (cpk cpk) (self self)
      (priv-stor priv-stor) (y y) (x x) (z 0) (z-0 1) (z-1 2)))
  (maps
    ((0 1 2)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (cpk cpk) (self self) (priv-stor priv-stor) (y y)
        (x x) (s s) (ca ca) (c c) (spk spk) (request request)
        (response response) (priv-stor-0 priv-stor-0))))
  (origs (pt (4 1)) (pt-0 (3 1)) (pt-1 (2 1)) (sr (0 2)))
  (ugens (spk (4 1)) (cpk (3 1)) (y (0 2))))

(comment "Nothing left to do")

(defprotocol tls13mtlspfs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (cpriv-stor locn))
    (trace (load cpriv-stor (pv c cpk (invk cpk)))
      (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x)
    (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-client-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "client" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-client-0
    (forall ((z strd) (c name) (cpk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c)) (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))

        (x expt) (z z-0 z-1 strd))

        (and (p "server" z 6) (p "" z-0 2) (p "privkey-disclose" z-1 3)
          (p "server" "cr" z cr) (p "server" "sr" z sr)
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "y" z y)

(defprotocol tls13mtlspfs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (cpriv-stor locn))
    (trace (load cpriv-stor (pv c cpk (invk cpk)))
      (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x)
    (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca c name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (cat c (invk cpk)
            (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            c (invk cpk) (enc (hash "client" c (invk cpk)) (privk ca)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-client-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "client" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-client-0
    (forall ((z strd) (c name) (cpk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c)) (gen-st (pv c cpk (invk cpk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))
